The prospectively frozen campaign ee91815afe069b316bf095abc7bdcaadb46eabcd3a2f3fff898fda712789af5a stopped on zero-based job 18 after eighteen complete paired jobs. Its original arm completed fifty factual cases; the first candidate session completed forty-five. The next candidate process exited before loading and before any request or reset. Its stdout is empty. The native error says that the physical ceiling plus three GB of actual reclaimable memory was unavailable. The coordinator stopped, the model processes exited and the original job and campaign remain incomplete. This is an execution failure, not a model-task failure or noninferiority result. No partial answer, grade distribution, paired statistic or quality verdict was inspected.

The parent recorded 24,142,446,592 reclaimable bytes before the rejected native launch and 24,136,695,808 afterwards. The frozen native preflight did not record its own value or distinguish an unavailable host-statistics read from insufficient bytes. The exact cause is therefore unresolved. A stale native host-statistics observation immediately after process release is plausible, because the app already waits past that cache interval before replanning; these receipts do not prove that cause. No memory budget is increased or safety check bypassed.

The current conversation owner now waits at least 1.05 seconds after the owned child has exited and its pipes/monitor have drained, then records a fresh parent snapshot. It applies once per child, including an unsuccessful session, and does not retry it. Native source separately records the actual preflight reading or its absence and the refusal in the incomplete receipt before returning. Twelve Python groups pass on each local runtime, including real child completion, premature EOF, watchdog/caller ownership, post-exit settling and failed termination. The native change is being built separately under the existing six-GB compiler-tree, nine-GB preflight and three-GB headroom limits; no model is running. Native compilation/acceptance is still pending at this checkpoint.

The original frozen files are untouched. The complete file custody inventory binds their exact bytes without interpreting partial outcomes. A continuation must be separately frozen before any new answers, preserve every completed answer and failed attempt, keep the original task selection/order/graders/margins and prevent replay of already answered tasks. No such continuation is launched or silently substituted here, and the old experiment is not declared successful. Actual product outcomes, full export, speed and integrated release gates remain open.

Complete engine CI 37225844505 now passes the earlier standalone loader commit 6763cd9f1ef7268f6809c4c7a0c1bc6ce29f51da, including optimized and instrumented catalogues, static/transport checks and external-library use. Its complete Mac CI was captured previously. This proves the source integration without model weights; the standalone allowlist remains empty pending actual export and physical proof.

Raw metadata, custody inventory, frozen protocol, new source/tests and completed prior CI follow. Home paths are normalized to <HOME>; original and normalized sizes and SHA-256 hashes remain explicit. Compression is verified by roundtrip. Partial answers remain only in their preserved original local run files and are not summarized here.
